They should answer the following questions:\n   - What exactly do I want to achieve?\n   - What are the key tasks or actions required to achieve this goal?\n   - Who is involved in achieving this goal?\n   - What resources are needed to achieve this goal?\n\n   *Example*: Instead of \"Improve team communication,\" consider \"Establish a weekly team meeting to discuss progress, challenges, and action items, with all team members attending and active participation encouraged.\"\n\n2. **Measurable**: To track progress and know when the goal is achieved, leadership goals should have quantifiable metrics.
